Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the geological object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 896. The moduli space has dimension at most 292 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by geological generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 896, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 292. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in geological theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 880 and the invariant I(A,B) = 7 mod 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the geological structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 7.
